<#
.SYNOPSIS
    PHOENIX REPAIR V7 - The Canon-Compliant Deployment Engine.

[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)]
    [string]$TargetEnv, # 'production' or 'staging'

    [switch]$DryRun,
    [switch]$ForceRedeploy
)

# --- 0. CONSTANTS & LAW ---
$ENERGY = 100

function Halt-System {

    Write-Error "⛔ SYSTEM HALT: $Reason"
    $global:ENERGY = 0
    Write-Host "::set-output name=deployment_status::BLOCKED"
    exit 1
}

# --- 1. ENVIRONMENT GATES (DEPLOYMENTCANONV1) ---
Write-Host "🔹 [GATE 1] Checking Environment..."

# G1.1: Node Version
$nodeVer = node -v
if ($nodeVer -notmatch "v18\.") {
    # UNKNOWN: Exact patch version not in canon, but v18 is law.
    Halt-System "Node version mismatch. Canon requires v18.x. Found: $nodeVer"
}

# G1.2: Wrangler Auth
$wranglerWho = npx wrangler whoami 2>&1
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    Halt-System "Wrangler not authenticated."
}

# G1.3: Phoenix Master Key
$masterKey = $env:PHOENIXMASTERKEY
if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($masterKey) -or $masterKey.Length -lt $MIN_KEY_LENGTH) {
    Halt-System "PHOENIXMASTERKEY missing or length < $MIN_KEY_LENGTH."
}

Write-Host "✅ [GATE 1] Environment Secure."

# --- 2. PRECHECKS (DRONECHECKLISTCANONV1) ---
Write-Host "🔹 [GATE 2] Running Prechecks..."

# P2.1: Verify Canon Hash (Stubbed - Logic requires local file read)
# In production, this hashes local ./canon/ files and compares to expected.
Write-Host "   > Verifying loadPhoenixCanon integrity..."
# if (Get-FileHash ./canon/law.json).Hash -ne $CANON_HASH_EXPECTED { Halt-System "Canon Hash Mismatch" }

# P2.2: Energy Check
if ($ENERGY -lt 50) {
    Halt-System "Energy Level ($ENERGY) too low for deployment."
}

Write-Host "✅ [GATE 2] Prechecks Passed."

# --- 3. DRY RUN (If requested) ---
if ($DryRun) {
    Write-Host "🔸 [DRY RUN] Simulating Deploy Steps..."
    Write-Host "   > Would backup bundle..."
    Write-Host "   > Would run wrangler deploy --env $TargetEnv --dry-run"
    Write-Host "   > Would verify /health"
    Write-Host "✅ [DRY RUN] Complete. No changes made."
    exit 0
}

# --- 4. DEPLOYMENT EXECUTION ---
Write-Host "🔹 [GATE 3] Executing Deployment..."

# S4.1: Create Backup
Write-Host "   > Creating .bak of current worker bundle..."
$timestamp = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd-HHmm"
Copy-Item "./dist/worker.js" "./dist/worker.js.bak-$timestamp" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
# D1 Snapshot logic would go here (UNKNOWN: Exact D1 backup command depends on exact schema)

# S4.2: Wrangler Deploy
Write-Host "   > Deploying to $TargetEnv..."
npx wrangler deploy --env $TargetEnv
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    Halt-System "Wrangler Deploy Failed. Initiating Rollback Protocol."
}

# --- 5. POST-CHECKS & VERIFICATION ---
Write-Host "🔹 [GATE 4] Verifying Deployment..."

# V5.1: Health Check
$workerUrl = if ($TargetEnv -eq "production") { "https://phoenix-api.com" } else { "https://staging.phoenix-api.com" }
try {
    $health = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$workerUrl/health" -Method Get -TimeoutSec 10
    if ($health.status -ne "OK") { throw "Health status not OK" }
} catch {
    Halt-System "Health Check Failed: $_. Initiating Rollback."
}

# V5.2: Ledger Verify Chain
try {
    $chainStatus = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$workerUrl/ledger/verifychain" -Method Get -Headers @{ "x-deathstar-key" = $masterKey }
    if ($chainStatus.ok -ne $true) { throw "Ledger Verify returned false" }
} catch {
    Halt-System "Ledger Verification Failed. Chain integrity compromised."
}

# V5.3: Audit & Log
Write-Host "   > Logging Audit Event..."
# Logic to POST /ledger/append with AUDIT_SUCCESS event would go here.

Write-Host "✅ [SUCCESS] Deployment Complete. Energy Steady at $ENERGY."


---

### **ARTIFACT 2: deploy-prod.ps1**

**Purpose:** The simple, dumb drone trigger. It wraps the complex repair script with specific production parameters, ensuring no human error in argument selection.

function Get-WorkerUrl {
  param([string]$Path = 'proof/workerurl.txt')
  if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { throw "MISSING: $Path" }
  $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim()
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }
  return $u.TrimEnd('/')
}

function Assert-NotUnknown {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Value,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Name)
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Value) -or $Value -match '^UNKNOWN') {
    throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: $Name is not set (value='$Value')"
  }
}

function Get-AidutConfig {
  $cfg = [ordered]@{
    AUTH_SCHEME      = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME      ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    AUTH_HEADER_NAME = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    AUTH_KEY         = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY         ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    APPEND_PATH      = ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH      ?? '/ledgerappend')
  }
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME'
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY    -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_KEY'
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name 'AIDUT_APPEND_PATH'
  return $cfg
}

function New-AidutHeaders {
  param([hashtable]$Cfg)
  $h = @{ 'content-type' = 'application/json' }

  switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) {
    'header' {
      Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME'
      $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY
      break
    }
    'bearer' {
      $h['Authorization'] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)"
      break
    }
    default {
      throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be 'header' or 'bearer' (got '$($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)')"
    }
  }
  return $h
}

function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  [CmdletBinding()]
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$AppendPath,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Headers,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Body,
    [int]$TimeoutSec = 5
  )

  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name 'APPEND_PATH'
  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress

  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec
    return [pscustomobject]@{
      ok     = $true
      status = [int]$res.StatusCode
      cache  = ($res.Headers.'x-cache')
      msg    = 'OK'
    }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    return [pscustomobject]@{
      ok     = $false
      status = $status
      cache  = $null
      msg    = ($_.Exception.Message.Split(':')[0])
    }
  }
}

function Write-DeterministicLog {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][object[]]$Rows,
        [string]$Header = $null)

  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
  if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }
  foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }

  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent
  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
  $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8
}

function Invoke-Concurrent {
  [CmdletBinding()]
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script,
        [int]$Throttle = 10)

  if ($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -ge 7) {
    return $Inputs | ForEach-Object -Parallel $Script -ThrottleLimit $Throttle
  }
  
  # Fallback for PS 5.1 / No Parallel
  $jobs = @()
  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {
      $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i
      if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) { $jobs | Receive-Job -AutoRemoveJob; $jobs = @() }
  }
  $results = Receive-Job -Job $jobs -Wait
  Remove-Job -Job $jobs -Force
  return $results
}


**STEP 3: CREATE tools/aidut/stage1-rate-limit.ps1**
*(Paste this V3 Code exactly)*

powershell
# tools/aidut/stage1-rate-limit.ps1 (V3)
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cfg      = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers  = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath  = 'proof/stage1-rate-limit.txt'

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage1'; blockid="RLT-$i"; nonce=$i; commandid="rlt-$i"; payload=@{seq=$i} }
  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 5
  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..11) -Script $script -Throttle 11

$lines = $results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object {
  if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $_.status, ($_.cache ?? '') }
  else       { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, ($_.status ?? ''), $_.msg }
}

$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE1 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) authScheme=$($cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) ps=$($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)"
Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header $meta

Get-Content $logPath -Tail 20

**ARTIFACT 2: tools/aidut/stage3-ultra.ps1**
*(The Idempotency Hammer)*

powershell
# tools/aidut/stage3-ultra.ps1 (V4)
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cfg      = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers  = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath  = 'proof/stage3-ultra.txt'

# Duplicate intent: same blockid+nonce repeatedly
$dupNonce  = 1
$dupBlock  = 'ULTRA-DUP-1'
$dupCmd    = 'ultra-dup-1'

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage3'; blockid=$using:dupBlock; nonce=$using:dupNonce; commandid=$using:dupCmd; payload=@{seq=$i; stage='ultra'; dup=$true} }
  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8
  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..1000) -Script $script -Throttle 150

$okCount   = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count
$failCount = @($results | Where-Object { -not $_.ok }).Count
$cacheStats = $results | Where-Object { $_.cache } | Group-Object cache | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "cache:$($_.Name) count:$($_.Count)" }

$lines = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
$lines.Add("AIDUT_STAGE3 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) authScheme=$($cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)")
$lines.Add("DUP nonce=$dupNonce blockid=$dupBlock")
$lines.Add("SUMMARY ok=$okCount fail=$failCount")
$lines.Add("CACHE")
foreach ($c in $cacheStats) { $lines.Add($c) }
$lines.Add("TAIL")
$lines.AddRange(
  ($results | Sort-Object i | Select-Object -Last 20 | ForEach-Object {
    if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $_.status, ($_.cache ?? '') }
    else       { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, ($_.status ?? ''), $_.msg }
  })
)

Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 40


**ARTIFACT 3: tools/aidut/setup-aidut.ps1**
*(The Validator)*

powershell
# tools/aidut/setup-aidut.ps1 (V4)
param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

Write-Host "🔹 SETUP: Validating Environment..."

# 1. Write URL Proof
$proofDir = "proof"
if (-not (Test-Path $proofDir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $proofDir | Out-Null }
$WorkerUrl | Set-Content -Path "$proofDir/workerurl.txt" -NoNewline
Write-Host "   > Written to proof/workerurl.txt"

# 2. Check Vars
$cfg = Get-AidutConfig # Throws if UNKNOWN
Write-Host "   > Config Valid: Scheme=$($cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)"

# 3. Smoke Test (One Append)
Write-Host "   > Firing Smoke Test (1 Request)..."
$headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$body    = @{ ledgerid='audit-setup'; blockid="SMOKE-$(Get-Random)"; nonce=99999; commandid="smoke"; payload=@{status='setup'} }
$r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $WorkerUrl -AppendPath $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $headers -Body $body
if ($r.ok) {
    Write-Host "✅ SETUP COMPLETE. Smoke Test Passed (200 OK)."
} else {
    Write-Error "⛔ SETUP FAILED. Smoke Test returned $($r.status): $($r.msg)"
}

### **1. tools/aidut/common.ps1 (The Core + Patch)**

powershell
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Get-WorkerUrl {
  param([string]$Path = 'proof/workerurl.txt')
  if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { throw "MISSING: $Path" }
  $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim()
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }
  return $u.TrimEnd('/')
}

function Assert-NotUnknown {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Value, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Name)
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Value) -or $Value -match '^UNKNOWN') {
    throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: $Name is not set (value='$Value')"
  }
}

function Get-AidutConfig {
  $cfg = [ordered]@{
    AUTH_SCHEME      = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME      ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    AUTH_HEADER_NAME = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    AUTH_KEY         = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY         ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    APPEND_PATH      = ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH      ?? '/ledgerappend')
  }
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME'
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY    -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_KEY'
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name 'AIDUT_APPEND_PATH'
  return $cfg
}

function New-AidutHeaders {
  param([hashtable]$Cfg)
  $h = @{ 'content-type' = 'application/json' }
  switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) {
    'header' {
      Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME'
      $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY
      break
    }
    'bearer' {
      $h['Authorization'] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)"
      break
    }
    default { throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be 'header' or 'bearer'" }
  }
  return $h
}

function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  [CmdletBinding()]
  param([string]$WorkerUrl, [string]$AppendPath, [hashtable]$Headers, [hashtable]$Body, [int]$TimeoutSec = 5)
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name 'APPEND_PATH'
  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=($res.Headers.'x-cache'); msg='OK' }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=($_.Exception.Message.Split(':')[0]) }
  }
}

function Write-DeterministicLog {
  param([string]$Path, [object[]]$Rows, [string]$Header=$null)
  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
  if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }
  foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }
  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent
  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
  $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8
}

function Invoke-Concurrent {
  [CmdletBinding()]
  param([int[]]$Inputs, [scriptblock]$Script, [int]$Throttle = 10)
  
  if ($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -ge 7) {
    return $Inputs | ForEach-Object -Parallel $Script -ThrottleLimit $Throttle
  }
  # Fallback for PS 5.1
  $jobs = @()
  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {
      $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i
      if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) { $jobs | Receive-Job -AutoRemoveJob; $jobs = @() }
  }
  $results = Receive-Job -Job $jobs -Wait
  Remove-Job -Job $jobs -Force
  return $results
}

### **2. tools/aidut/stage1-rate-limit.ps1**

powershell
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
$cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath = 'proof/stage1-rate-limit.txt'

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage1'; blockid="RLT-$i"; nonce=$i; commandid="rlt-$i"; payload=@{seq=$i} }
  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 5
  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..11) -Script $script -Throttle 11
$lines = $results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i,$_.status,($_.cache??'') } else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i,($_.status??''),$_.msg } }
Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header "AIDUT_STAGE1 workerUrl=$workerUrl ps=$($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)"
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 20


---

### **3. tools/aidut/stage2-injection.ps1**

powershell
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
$cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath = 'proof/stage2-injection.txt'

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage2'; blockid="INJ-$i"; nonce=$i; commandid="inj-$i"; payload=@{seq=$i; stage='injection'} }
  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8
  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; msg=$r.msg }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..100) -Script $script -Throttle 100
$ok = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count; $fail = @($results | Where-Object {!$_.ok}).Count
$lines = @("SUMMARY ok=$ok fail=$fail", "DETAILS") + ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1}" -f $_.i,$_.status } else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i,($_.status??''),$_.msg } })
Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header "AIDUT_STAGE2 workerUrl=$workerUrl"
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 30


---

### **4. tools/aidut/stage3-ultra.ps1**

powershell
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
$cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath = 'proof/stage3-ultra.txt'
$dupBlock = 'ULTRA-DUP-1'

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage3'; blockid=$using:dupBlock; nonce=1; commandid='ultra-dup-1'; payload=@{seq=$i; stage='ultra'; dup=$true} }
  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8
  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..1000) -Script $script -Throttle 150
$ok = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count; $fail = @($results | Where-Object {!$_.ok}).Count
$cache = $results | Where-Object { $_.cache } | Group-Object cache | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "cache:$($_.Name) count:$($_.Count)" }
$lines = @("SUMMARY ok=$ok fail=$fail", "CACHE") + $cache + @("TAIL") + ($results | Sort-Object i | Select-Object -Last 20 | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i,$_.status,($_.cache??'') } else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i,($_.status??''),$_.msg } })
Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header "AIDUT_STAGE3 workerUrl=$workerUrl"
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 40

### **5. tools/aidut/setup-aidut.ps1 (The Gate)**

powershell
param([string]$WorkerUrl)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'; . "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
Write-Host "🔹 SETUP: Validating Environment..."
if (-not (Test-Path "proof")) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path "proof" | Out-Null }
$WorkerUrl | Set-Content -Path "proof/workerurl.txt" -NoNewline
$cfg = Get-AidutConfig
Write-Host "   > Firing Smoke Test..."
$headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$body = @{ ledgerid='audit-setup'; blockid="SMOKE-$(Get-Random)"; nonce=99999; commandid="smoke"; payload=@{status='setup'} }
$r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $WorkerUrl -AppendPath $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $headers -Body $body
if ($r.ok) { Write-Host "✅ SETUP COMPLETE. Smoke Test Passed (200 OK)." } else { Write-Error "⛔ SETUP FAILED. Smoke Test returned $($r.status): $($r.msg)" }

'/ledgerappend')\n  }\n  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME'\n  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY    -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_KEY'\n  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name 'AIDUT_APPEND_PATH'\n  return $cfg\n}\n\nfunction New-AidutHeaders {\n  param([hashtable]$Cfg)\n  $h = @{ 'content-type' = 'application/json' }\n  switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) {\n    'header' {\n      Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME'\n      $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY\n      break\n    }\n    'bearer' {\n      $h['Authorization'] = \"Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)\"\n      break\n    }\n    default { throw \"UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be 'header' or 'bearer'\" }\n  }\n  return $h\n}\n\nfunction Invoke-LedgerAppend {\n  [CmdletBinding()]\n  param([string]$WorkerUrl, [string]$AppendPath, [hashtable]$Headers, [hashtable]$Body, [int]$TimeoutSec = 5)\n  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name 'APPEND_PATH'\n  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress\n  try {\n    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri \"$WorkerUrl$AppendPath\" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec\n    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=($res.Headers.'x-cache'); msg='OK' }\n  } catch {\n    $status = $null\n    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}\n    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=($_.Exception.Message.Split(':')[0]) }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction Write-DeterministicLog {\n  param([string]$Path, [object[]]$Rows, [string]$Header=$null)\n  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]\n  if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }\n  foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }\n  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent\n  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }\n  $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8\n}\n\nfunction Invoke-Concurrent {\n  [CmdletBinding()]\n  param([int[]]$Inputs, [scriptblock]$Script, [int]$Throttle = 10)\n  \n  if ($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -ge 7) {\n    return $Inputs | ForEach-Object -Parallel $Script -ThrottleLimit $Throttle\n  }\n  # Fallback for PS 5.1\n  $jobs = @()\n  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {\n      $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i\n      if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) { $jobs | Receive-Job -AutoRemoveJob; $jobs = @() }\n  }\n  $results = Receive-Job -Job $jobs -Wait\n  Remove-Job -Job $jobs -Force\n  return $results\n}\n",
          "tools/aidut/stage1-rate-limit.ps1": ". \"$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1\"\n$cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg\n$logPath = 'proof/stage1-rate-limit.txt'\n\n$script = {\n  param($i)\n  . \"$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1\"\n  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage1'; blockid=\"RLT-$i\"; nonce=$i; commandid=\"rlt-$i\"; payload=@{seq=$i} }\n  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 5\n  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg }\n}\n\n$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..11) -Script $script -Throttle 11\n$lines = $results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { \"{0} OK {1} {2}\" -f $_.i,$_.status,($_.cache??'') } else { \"{0} FAIL {1} {2}\" -f $_.i,($_.status??''),$_.msg } }\nWrite-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header \"AIDUT_STAGE1 workerUrl=$workerUrl ps=$($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)\"\nGet-Content $logPath -Tail 20\n",
          "tools/aidut/stage2-injection.ps1": ". \"$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1\"\n$cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg\n$logPath = 'proof/stage2-injection.txt'\n\n$script = {\n  param($i)\n  . \"$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1\"\n  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage2'; blockid=\"INJ-$i\"; nonce=$i; commandid=\"inj-$i\"; payload=@{seq=$i; stage='injection'} }\n  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8\n  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; msg=$r.msg }\n}\n\n$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..100) -Script $script -Throttle 100\n$ok = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count; $fail = @($results | Where-Object {!$_.ok}).Count\n$lines = @(\"SUMMARY ok=$ok fail=$fail\", \"DETAILS\") + ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { \"{0} OK {1}\" -f $_.i,$_.status } else { \"{0} FAIL {1} {2}\" -f $_.i,($_.status??''),$_.msg } })\nWrite-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header \"AIDUT_STAGE2 workerUrl=$workerUrl\"\nGet-Content $logPath -Tail 30\n",

          "tools/aidut/stage2-injection.ps1": ". \"$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1\"\n\n$cfg       = Get-AidutConfig\n$workerUrl  = Get-WorkerUrl\n$headers   = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg\n$logPath   = 'proof/stage2-injection.txt'\n\n$script = {\n  param($i)\n  . \"$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1\"\n\n  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage2'; blockid=\"INJ-$i\"; nonce=$i; commandid=\"inj-$i\"; payload=@{seq=$i; stage='injection'} }\n  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8\n  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; msg=$r.msg }\n}\n\n$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..100) -Script $script -Throttle 100\n\n$okCount   = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count\n$failCount = @($results | Where-Object { -not $_.ok }).Count\n\n$lines = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]\n$lines.Add(\"AIDUT_STAGE2 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) authScheme=$($cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)\")\n$lines.Add(\"SUMMARY ok=$okCount fail=$failCount\")\n$lines.Add('DETAILS')\n$lines.AddRange(\n  ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object {\n    if ($_.ok) { \"{0} OK {1}\" -f $_.i, $_.status }\n    else       { \"{0} FAIL {1} {2}\" -f $_.i, ($_.status ?? powershell# Create tools/aidut directory
mkdir tools/aidut -Force | Out-Null

# Create common.ps1
@'
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Get-WorkerUrl {
  param([string]$Path = 'proof/workerurl.txt')
  if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { throw "MISSING: $Path" }
  $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim()
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }
  return $u.TrimEnd('/')
}

function Assert-NotUnknown {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Value,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Name)
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Value) -or $Value -match '^UNKNOWN') {
    throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: $Name is not set (value='$Value')"
  }
}

function Get-AidutConfig {
  $cfg = [ordered]@{
    AUTH_SCHEME      = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME      ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    AUTH_HEADER_NAME = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    AUTH_KEY         = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY         ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    APPEND_PATH      = ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH      ?? '/ledgerappend')
  }
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME'
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY    -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_KEY'
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name 'AIDUT_APPEND_PATH'
  return $cfg
}

function New-AidutHeaders {
  param([hashtable]$Cfg)
  $h = @{ 'content-type' = 'application/json' }

  switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) {
    'bearer' {
      $h['Authorization'] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)"
      break
    }
    'header' {
      Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME'
      $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY
      break
    }
    default {
      throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be 'bearer' or 'header' (got '$($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)')"
    }
  }
  return $h
}

function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  [CmdletBinding()]
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$AppendPath,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Headers,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Body,
    [int]$TimeoutSec = 5
  )

  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name 'APPEND_PATH'
  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress

  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=($res.Headers.'x-cache'); msg='OK' }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=($_.Exception.Message.Split(':')[0]) }
  }
}

function Write-DeterministicLog {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][object[]]$Rows,
        [string]$Header = $null)

  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
  if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }
  foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }

  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent
  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
  $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8
}

function Invoke-Concurrent {
  [CmdletBinding()]
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script,
        [int]$Throttle = 10)

  if ($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -ge 7) {
    return $Inputs | ForEach-Object -Parallel $Script -ThrottleLimit $Throttle
  }

  # Fallback for PS 5.1
  $jobs = @()
  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {
    $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i
    if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) {
      $jobs | Receive-Job -AutoRemoveJob
      $jobs = @()
    }
  }
  $results = Receive-Job -Job $jobs -Wait
  Remove-Job -Job $jobs -Force
  return $results
}
'@ | Set-Content -Path tools/aidut/common.ps1 -Encoding utf8

# Create setup-aidut.ps1
@'
param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

if (-not (Test-Path 'proof')) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path 'proof' | Out-Null }
$WorkerUrl | Set-Content -Path 'proof/workerurl.txt' -NoNewline

function Invoke-Smoke {
  param([string]$Scheme, [string]$HeaderName)

  $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME = $Scheme
  if ($Scheme -eq 'header') {
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($HeaderName)) { throw 'HeaderName required for header scheme' }
    $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME = $HeaderName
  }

  $cfg     = Get-AidutConfig
  $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
  $body    = @{ ledgerid='audit-setup'; blockid="SMOKE-$(Get-Random)"; nonce=99999; commandid='smoke'; payload=@{status='setup'} }
  return Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $WorkerUrl -AppendPath $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8
}

Write-Host 'AIDUT_SETUP: smoke-gate start'

# Attempt order per MASTER_CREDENTIALS_VAULT.md:
# 1) bearer
# 2) header fallback x-phoenix-secret

$r1 = Invoke-Smoke -Scheme 'bearer' -HeaderName ''
if ($r1.ok) {
  Write-Host 'AIDUT_SETUP_OK: bearer'
  exit 0
}

$r2 = Invoke-Smoke -Scheme 'header' -HeaderName 'x-phoenix-secret'
if ($r2.ok) {
  Write-Host 'AIDUT_SETUP_OK: header:x-phoenix-secret'
  exit 0
}

Write-Error ("AIDUT_SETUP_FAIL: bearer={0} status={1} msg={2} | header:x-phoenix-secret={3} status={4} msg={5}" -f $r1.ok,$r1.status,$r1.msg,$r2.ok,$r2.status,$r2.msg)
exit 1
'@ | Set-Content -Path tools/aidut/setup-aidut.ps1 -Encoding utf8

# Create stage1-rate-limit.ps1
@'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cfg       = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl  = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers   = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath   = 'proof/stage1-rate-limit.txt'

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

  $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage1'; blockid="RLT-$i"; nonce=$i; commandid="rlt-$i"; payload=@{seq=$i} }
  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 5
  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..11) -Script $script -Throttle 11

$lines = $results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object {
  if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $_.status, ($_.cache ?? '') }
  else       { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, ($_.status ?? ''), $_.msg }
}

$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE1 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) authScheme=$($cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) ps=$($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)"
Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header $meta

Get-Content $logPath -Tail 20
'@ | Set-Content -Path tools/aidut/stage1-rate-limit.ps1 -Encoding utf8

# Create stage2-injection.ps1
@'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cfg       = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl  = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers   = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath   = 'proof/stage2-injection.txt'

$script = {

  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cacheStats = $results | Where-Object { $_.cache } |

$lines.Add("DUP nonce=$dupNonce blockid=$dupBlock")
$lines.Add("SUMMARY ok=$okCount fail=$failCount")

foreach ($c in $cacheStats) { $lines.Add($c) }
$lines.Add('TAIL')
$lines.AddRange(
  ($results | Sort-Object i | Select-Object -Last 20 | ForEach-Object {
    if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $_.status, ($_.cache ?? '') }
    else       { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, ($_.status ?? ''), $_.msg }

)

Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Get-WorkerUrl {
  param([string]$Path = 'proof/workerurl.txt')
  if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { throw "MISSING: $Path" }
  $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim()
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }
  return $u.TrimEnd('/')
}

function Assert-NotUnknown {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Value,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Name)
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Value) -or $Value -match '^UNKNOWN') {
    throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: $Name is not set (value='$Value')"
  }
}

function Get-AidutConfig {
  $cfg = [ordered]@{
    AUTH_SCHEME      = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME      ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    AUTH_HEADER_NAME = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    AUTH_KEY         = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY         ?? 'UNKNOWN_MUST_SET')
    APPEND_PATH      = ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH      ?? '/ledgerappend')
  }
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME'
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY    -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_KEY'
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name 'AIDUT_APPEND_PATH'
  return $cfg
}

function New-AidutHeaders {
  param([hashtable]$Cfg)
  $h = @{ 'content-type' = 'application/json' }

  switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) {
    'bearer' {
      $h['Authorization'] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)"
      break
    }
    'header' {
      Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name 'AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME'
      $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY
      break
    }
    default {
      throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be 'bearer' or 'header' (got '$($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)')"
    }
  }
  return $h
}

function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  [CmdletBinding()]
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$AppendPath,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Headers,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Body,
    [int]$TimeoutSec = 5
  )

  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name 'APPEND_PATH'
  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress

  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=($res.Headers.'x-cache'); msg='OK' }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=($_.Exception.Message.Split(':')[0]) }
  }
}

function Write-DeterministicLog {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][object[]]$Rows,
        [string]$Header = $null)

  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
  if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }
  foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }

  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent
  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
  $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8
}

function Invoke-Concurrent {
  [CmdletBinding()]
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script,
        [int]$Throttle = 10)

  if ($PSVersionTable.PSVersion.Major -ge 7) {
    return $Inputs | ForEach-Object -Parallel $Script -ThrottleLimit $Throttle
  }

  $jobs = @()
  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {
    $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i
    if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) {
      $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output
      $jobs = @()
    }
  }
  if ($jobs.Count -gt 0) {
    $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output
  }
}
'@

function Invoke-Smoke {
  param([string]$Scheme, [string]$HeaderName)

  $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME = $Scheme
  if ($Scheme -eq 'header') {
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($HeaderName)) { throw 'HeaderName required for header scheme' }
    $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME = $HeaderName
  }

  $cfg     = Get-AidutConfig
  $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
  $body    = @{ ledgerid='audit-setup'; blockid="SMOKE-$(Get-Random)"; nonce=99999; commandid='smoke'; payload=@{status='setup'} }
  return Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $WorkerUrl -AppendPath $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8
}

  exit 0

if ($r2.ok) {
  Write-Host 'AIDUT_SETUP_OK: header:x-phoenix-secret'
  exit 0
}

exit 1

  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=($res.Headers.'x-cache'); msg='OK' }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=($_.Exception.Message.Split(':')[0]) }
  }
}

function Write-DeterministicLog {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][object[]]$Rows, [string]$Header = $null)
  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
  if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }
  foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }
  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent
  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
  $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8
}

function Invoke-Concurrent {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script, [int]$Throttle = 10)
  $jobs = @()
  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {
    $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i
    if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output; $jobs = @() }
  }
  if ($jobs.Count -gt 0) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output }
}

# Create setup
@'
param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
if (-not (Test-Path 'proof')) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path 'proof' | Out-Null }
$WorkerUrl | Set-Content -Path 'proof/workerurl.txt' -NoNewline
function Invoke-Smoke { param([string]$Scheme, [string]$HeaderName); $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME = $Scheme; if ($Scheme -eq 'header') { if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($HeaderName)) { throw 'HeaderName required' }; $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME = $HeaderName }; $cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg; $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-setup'; blockid="SMOKE-$(Get-Random)"; nonce=99999; commandid='smoke'; payload=@{status='setup'} }; return Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $WorkerUrl -AppendPath $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8 }
Write-Host 'AIDUT_SETUP: smoke-gate start'
$r1 = Invoke-Smoke -Scheme 'bearer' -HeaderName ''
if ($r1.ok) { Write-Host 'AIDUT_SETUP_OK: bearer'; exit 0 }
$r2 = Invoke-Smoke -Scheme 'header' -HeaderName 'x-phoenix-secret'
if ($r2.ok) { Write-Host 'AIDUT_SETUP_OK: header:x-phoenix-secret'; exit 0 }
Write-Error "AIDUT_SETUP_FAIL: both failed"; exit 1
'@ | Set-Content tools/aidut/setup-aidut.ps1

# Create stage1
@'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
$cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg; $logPath = 'proof/stage1-rate-limit.txt'
$script = { param($i); . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"; $body = @{ ledgerid='audit-stage1'; blockid="RLT-$i"; nonce=$i; commandid="rlt-$i"; payload=@{seq=$i} }; $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 5; [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg } }
$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..11) -Script $script -Throttle 11
$lines = $results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $_.status, ($_.cache ?? '') } else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, ($_.status ?? ''), $_.msg } }
$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE1 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) authScheme=$($cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)"
Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header $meta
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 20

powershell# Common.ps1
Set-Content tools/aidut/common.ps1 -Value 'Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
function Get-WorkerUrl { param([string]$Path = "proof/workerurl.txt"); if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { throw "MISSING: $Path" }; $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim(); if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }; return $u.TrimEnd("/") }
function Assert-NotUnknown { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Value, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Name); if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Value) -or $Value -match "^UNKNOWN") { throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: $Name is not set (value=''$Value'')" } }
function Get-AidutConfig { $cfg = [ordered]@{ AUTH_SCHEME = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME ?? "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET"); AUTH_HEADER_NAME = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME ?? "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET"); AUTH_KEY = ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY ?? "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET"); APPEND_PATH = ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH ?? "/ledgerappend") }; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME"; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_KEY"; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name "AIDUT_APPEND_PATH"; return $cfg }
function New-AidutHeaders { param([hashtable]$Cfg); $h = @{ "content-type" = "application/json" }; switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) { "bearer" { $h["Authorization"] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)"; break } "header" { Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME"; $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY; break } default { throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be ''bearer'' or ''header'' (got ''$($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)'')" } }; return $h }
function Invoke-LedgerAppend { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$AppendPath, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Headers, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Body, [int]$TimeoutSec = 5); Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name "APPEND_PATH"; $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress; try { $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec; return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=($res.Headers."x-cache"); msg="OK" } } catch { $status = $null; try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}; return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=($_.Exception.Message.Split(":")[0]) } } }
function Write-DeterministicLog { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][object[]]$Rows, [string]$Header = $null); $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]; if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }; foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }; $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent; if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }; $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8 }
function Invoke-Concurrent { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script, [int]$Throttle = 10); $jobs = @(); foreach ($i in $Inputs) { $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i; if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output; $jobs = @() } }; if ($jobs.Count -gt 0) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output } }'

>>pasted7:17 AMIdentified compatibility issue and devised solution.The user is running PowerShell 5.1 which doesn't support the ?? null coalescing operator. I need to rewrite the scripts to be compatible with PowerShell 5.1.🎯 POWERSHELL 5.1 COMPATIBILITY FIX
Your PowerShell version doesn't support the ?? operator. Let me fix the scripts:
powershell# Fix common.ps1
Set-Content tools/aidut/common.ps1 -Value @'
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
function Get-WorkerUrl { param([string]$Path = "proof/workerurl.txt"); if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { throw "MISSING: $Path" }; $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim(); if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }; return $u.TrimEnd("/") }
function Assert-NotUnknown { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Value, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Name); if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Value) -or $Value -match "^UNKNOWN") { throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: $Name is not set (value='$Value')" } }
function Get-AidutConfig { $cfg = [ordered]@{ AUTH_SCHEME = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }; AUTH_HEADER_NAME = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }; AUTH_KEY = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }; APPEND_PATH = if ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH) { $env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH } else { "/ledgerappend" } }; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME"; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_KEY"; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name "AIDUT_APPEND_PATH"; return $cfg }
function New-AidutHeaders { param([hashtable]$Cfg); $h = @{ "content-type" = "application/json" }; switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) { "bearer" { $h["Authorization"] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)"; break } "header" { Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME"; $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY; break } default { throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be 'bearer' or 'header' (got '$($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)')" } }; return $h }
function Invoke-LedgerAppend { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$AppendPath, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Headers, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Body, [int]$TimeoutSec = 5); Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name "APPEND_PATH"; $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress; try { $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec; return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=$res.Headers["x-cache"]; msg="OK" } } catch { $status = $null; try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}; return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=($_.Exception.Message.Split(":")[0]) } } }
function Write-DeterministicLog { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][object[]]$Rows, [string]$Header = $null); $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]; if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }; foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }; $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent; if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }; $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8 }
function Invoke-Concurrent { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script, [int]$Throttle = 10); $jobs = @(); foreach ($i in $Inputs) { $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i; if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output; $jobs = @() } }; if ($jobs.Count -gt 0) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output } }
'@

# Fix stage1
Set-Content tools/aidut/stage1-rate-limit.ps1 -Value @'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
$cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg; $logPath = "proof/stage1-rate-limit.txt"
$script = { param($i); . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"; $body = @{ ledgerid="audit-stage1"; blockid="RLT-$i"; nonce=$i; commandid="rlt-$i"; payload=@{seq=$i} }; $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 5; [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg } }
$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..11) -Script $script -Throttle 11
$lines = $results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $_.status, $(if ($_.cache) { $_.cache } else { "" }) } else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.status) { $_.status } else { "" }), $_.msg } }
$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE1 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) authScheme=$($cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)"
Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header $meta
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 20
'@

powershell
# Update setup-aidut.ps1 (x-sovereign-key only)
Set-Content tools/aidut/setup-aidut.ps1 -Value @'
param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl)
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
if (-not (Test-Path "proof")) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path "proof" | Out-Null }
$WorkerUrl | Set-Content -Path "proof/workerurl.txt" -NoNewline
function Invoke-Smoke { param([string]$Scheme, [string]$HeaderName); $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME = $Scheme; if ($Scheme -eq "header") { if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($HeaderName)) { throw "HeaderName required" }; $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME = $HeaderName }; $cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg; $body = @{ ledger_id="audit-setup"; block_id="SMOKE-$(Get-Random)"; timestamp_utc=(Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"); author="AIDUT"; mode="EXECUTION"; block_type="TEST"; payload=@{status="setup"}; nonce=99999; protocol="PHOENIX/1.3"; command_id="smoke" } | ConvertTo-Json; return Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $WorkerUrl -AppendPath $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8 }
Write-Host "AIDUT_SETUP: smoke-gate start"
$r1 = Invoke-Smoke -Scheme "header" -HeaderName "x-sovereign-key"
if ($r1.ok) { Write-Host "AIDUT_SETUP_OK: header:x-sovereign-key"; exit 0 }
Write-Error "AIDUT_SETUP_FAIL"; exit 1
'@

# Update stage1 (correct schema)
Set-Content tools/aidut/stage1-rate-limit.ps1 -Value @'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"
$cfg = Get-AidutConfig; $workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl; $headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg; $logPath = "proof/stage1-rate-limit.txt"
$script = { param($i); . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"; $body = @{ ledger_id="audit-stage1"; block_id="RLT-$i"; timestamp_utc=(Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"); author="AIDUT"; mode="EXECUTION"; block_type="TEST"; payload=@{seq=$i}; nonce=$i; protocol="PHOENIX/1.3"; command_id="rlt-$i" } | ConvertTo-Json; $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 5; [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg } }
$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..11) -Script $script -Throttle 11
$lines = $results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $_.status, $(if ($_.cache) { $_.cache } else { "" }) } else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.status) { $_.status } else { "" }), $_.msg } }
$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE1 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) authScheme=$($cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)"
Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines -Header $meta
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 20
'@

"$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"; $body = @{ ledger_id="audit-stage2"; block_id="INJ-$i"; timestamp_utc=(Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"); author="AIDUT"; mode="EXECUTION"; block_type="TEST"; payload=@{seq=$i}; nonce=$i; protocol="PHOENIX/1.3"; command_id="inj-$i" } | ConvertTo-Json; $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec 8; [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; msg=$r.msg } }

Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 30

function Assert-NotUnknown { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Value, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Name); if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Value) -or $Value -match "^UNKNOWN") { throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: $Name is not set (value='$Value')" } }
function Get-AidutConfig { $cfg = [ordered]@{ AUTH_SCHEME = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }; AUTH_HEADER_NAME = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }; AUTH_KEY = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }; APPEND_PATH = if ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH) { $env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH } else { "/ledgerappend" } }; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME"; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_KEY"; Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name "AIDUT_APPEND_PATH"; return $cfg }
function New-AidutHeaders { param([hashtable]$Cfg); $h = @{ "content-type" = "application/json" }; switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) { "bearer" { $h["Authorization"] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)"; break } "header" { Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME"; $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY; break } default { throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be 'bearer' or 'header' (got '$($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)')" } }; return $h }

function Invoke-Concurrent { param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script, [int]$Throttle = 10); $jobs = @(); foreach ($i in $Inputs) { $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i; if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output; $jobs = @() } }; if ($jobs.Count -gt 0) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output } }

PATCH 1/2 — tools/aidut/common.ps1 (adds retry helper + keeps -UseBasicParsing)

Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

function Get-WorkerUrl {
  param([string]$Path = "proof/workerurl.txt")
  if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { throw "MISSING: $Path" }
  $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim()
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }
  return $u.TrimEnd("/")
}

function Assert-NotUnknown {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Value, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Name)
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Value) -or $Value -match "^UNKNOWN") {
    throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: $Name is not set (value='$Value')"
  }
}

function Get-AidutConfig {
  $cfg = [ordered]@{
    AUTH_SCHEME      = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }
    AUTH_HEADER_NAME = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }
    AUTH_KEY         = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY } else { "UNKNOWN_MUST_SET" }
    APPEND_PATH      = if ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH) { $env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH } else { "/ledgerappend" }
  }
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME"
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.AUTH_KEY -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_KEY"
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Name "AIDUT_APPEND_PATH"
  return $cfg
}

function New-AidutHeaders {
  param([hashtable]$Cfg)
  $h = @{ "content-type" = "application/json" }
  switch ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME) {
    "bearer" {
      $h["Authorization"] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)"
      break
    }
    "header" {
      Assert-NotUnknown -Value $Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME -Name "AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME"
      $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY
      break
    }
    default {
      throw "UNKNOWN_BLOCK: AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be 'bearer' or 'header' (got '$($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME)')"
    }
  }
  return $h
}

function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$AppendPath,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Headers,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Body,
    [int]$TimeoutSec = 5
  )
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name "APPEND_PATH"
  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec -UseBasicParsing
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=$res.Headers["x-cache"]; msg="OK" }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=($_.Exception.Message.Split(":")[0]) }
  }
}

function Write-DeterministicLog {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][object[]]$Rows, [string]$Header = $null)
  $out = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
  if ($Header) { $out.Add($Header) }
  foreach ($r in $Rows) { $out.Add([string]$r) }
  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent
  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
  $out | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8
}

function Invoke-Concurrent {
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script,
    [int]$Throttle = 10
  )
  $jobs = @()
  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {
    $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i
    if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) {
      $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output
      $jobs = @()
    }
  }
  if ($jobs.Count -gt 0) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output }
}

# V4.1 retry helper (Stage2 uses this)
function Invoke-WithRetry {
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Fn,
    [int]$MaxAttempts = 4,
    [int]$BaseDelayMs = 250
  )

  for ($attempt=1; $attempt -le $MaxAttempts; $attempt++) {
    $r = & $Fn
    if ($r -and $r.ok) { return $r }

    $status = $null
    try { $status = [int]$r.status } catch {}

    # Retry on: timeout/unknown (status null), 429, 503, 504, and transient web errors
    $retryable = $false
    if ($null -eq $status) { $retryable = $true }
    elseif ($status -in 429,503,504) { $retryable = $true }
    elseif ($r.msg -match "timed out|timeout|temporarily|connection|An error occurred") { $retryable = $true }

    if (-not $retryable -or $attempt -eq $MaxAttempts) { return $r }

    # exponential backoff + jitter
    $jitter = Get-Random -Minimum 0 -Maximum 200
    $delay = [Math]::Min(5000, ($BaseDelayMs * [Math]::Pow(2, ($attempt-1))) + $jitter)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $delay
  }
}
PATCH 2/2 — tools/aidut/stage2-injection.ps1 (tuned concurrency + retries + summary)

. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cfg = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath = "proof/stage2-injection.txt"

# Tunables (V4.1)
$THROTTLE = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_THROTTLE) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_THROTTLE } else { 20 }
$TIMEOUT  = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC } else { 25 }
$ATTEMPTS = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_ATTEMPTS) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_ATTEMPTS } else { 4 }

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

  $body = @{
    ledger_id      = "audit-stage2"
    block_id       = "INJ-$i"
    timestamp_utc  = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
    author         = "AIDUT"
    mode           = "EXECUTION"
    block_type     = "TEST"
    payload        = @{ seq = $i }
    nonce          = $i
    protocol       = "PHOENIX/1.3"
    command_id     = "inj-$i"
  }

  $fn = {
    Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec $using:TIMEOUT
  }

  $r = Invoke-WithRetry -Fn $fn -MaxAttempts $using:ATTEMPTS -BaseDelayMs 250

  [pscustomobject]@{
    i        = [int]$i
    ok       = [bool]$r.ok
    status   = $r.status
    cache    = $r.cache
    msg      = $r.msg
  }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..100) -Script $script -Throttle $THROTTLE

$okCount   = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count
$failCount = @($results | Where-Object { -not $_.ok }).Count

# status buckets
$bucket = @{}
foreach ($x in $results) {
  $k = if ($x.ok) { "200" } elseif ($null -eq $x.status) { "null" } else { [string]$x.status }
  if (-not $bucket.ContainsKey($k)) { $bucket[$k] = 0 }
  $bucket[$k]++
}
$bucketLine = ($bucket.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Value)" }) -join " "

$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE2_V41 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) throttle=$THROTTLE timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT attempts=$ATTEMPTS"
$lines = @()
$lines += $meta
$lines += "SUMMARY ok=$okCount fail=$failCount buckets: $bucketLine"
$lines += ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object {
  if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK 200 {1}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.cache) { $_.cache } else { "" }) }
  else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.status) { $_.status } else { "null" }), $_.msg }
})

Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines
Get-Content $logPath -Tail 30

Run Stage2 “VISIBLE MODE” (no jobs, no freeze) 👇

# Create a visible, non-job Stage2 runner
Set-Content .\stage2-visible.ps1 -Value @'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cfg = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg

# Tunables
$N        = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_N) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_N } else { 100 }
$TIMEOUT  = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC } else { 12 }

# Always write logs to repo-root proof/
$ROOT = Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\..")
$PROOF = Join-Path $ROOT "proof"
if (-not (Test-Path $PROOF)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $PROOF | Out-Null }
$logPath = Join-Path $PROOF "stage2-injection.txt"

$results = @()
$bucket = @{}

Write-Host "STAGE2_VISIBLE start: N=$N timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT url=$workerUrl path=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH)" -ForegroundColor Cyan

for ($i=1; $i -le $N; $i++) {
  $body = @{
    ledger_id     = "audit-stage2"
    block_id      = "INJ-$i"
    timestamp_utc = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
    author        = "AIDUT"
    mode          = "EXECUTION"
    block_type    = "TEST"
    payload       = @{ seq = $i }
    nonce         = $i
    protocol      = "PHOENIX/1.3"
    command_id    = "inj-$i"
  }

  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $workerUrl -AppendPath $cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec $TIMEOUT
  $ok = [bool]$r.ok
  $status = $r.status

  $k = if ($ok) { "200" } elseif ($null -eq $status) { "null" } else { [string]$status }
  if (-not $bucket.ContainsKey($k)) { $bucket[$k] = 0 }
  $bucket[$k]++

  $results += [pscustomobject]@{ i=$i; ok=$ok; status=$status; cache=$r.cache; msg=$r.msg }

  if (($i % 5) -eq 0) {
    Write-Host ("progress {0}/{1} buckets: {2}" -f $i, $N, (($bucket.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Value)" }) -join " ")) -ForegroundColor Yellow
  }
}

$okCount   = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count
$failCount = @($results | Where-Object { -not $_.ok }).Count
$bucketLine = ($bucket.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Value)" }) -join " "

$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE2_VISIBLE workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) N=$N timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT"
$lines = @()
$lines += $meta
$lines += "SUMMARY ok=$okCount fail=$failCount buckets: $bucketLine"
$lines += ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object {
  if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK 200 {1}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.cache) { $_.cache } else { "" }) }
  else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.status) { $_.status } else { "null" }), $_.msg }
})

Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines
Write-Host "WROTE: $logPath" -ForegroundColor Green
Get-Content $logPath | Select-String "SUMMARY"
'@ -Encoding utf8

# Run it
. .\stage2-visible.ps1

cd C:\phoenix\tools\aidut

# Ensure worker url exists where common.ps1 expects it (relative to cwd)
mkdir .\proof -Force | Out-Null
"https://phoenix-empire.mrmichaelhobbs123.workers.dev" | Set-Content .\proof\workerurl.txt -NoNewline

# Optional tunables (sane defaults for PS 5.1)
$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_THROTTLE   = "20"
$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC = "25"
$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_ATTEMPTS   = "4"

# PATCH stage2 to: lower throttle + retry + print status buckets + write SUMMARY
Set-Content .\stage2-injection.ps1 -Value @'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cfg = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath = "proof/stage2-injection.txt"

$THROTTLE = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_THROTTLE) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_THROTTLE } else { 20 }
$TIMEOUT  = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC } else { 25 }
$ATTEMPTS = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_ATTEMPTS) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_ATTEMPTS } else { 4 }

Write-Host "STAGE2_V41 start throttle=$THROTTLE timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT attempts=$ATTEMPTS url=$workerUrl path=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH)" -ForegroundColor Cyan

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

  $body = @{
    ledger_id     = "audit-stage2"
    block_id      = "INJ-$i"
    timestamp_utc = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
    author        = "AIDUT"
    mode          = "EXECUTION"
    block_type    = "TEST"
    payload       = @{ seq = $i }
    nonce         = $i
    protocol      = "PHOENIX/1.3"
    command_id    = "inj-$i"
  }

  $fn = {
    Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec $using:TIMEOUT
  }

  $r = Invoke-WithRetry -Fn $fn -MaxAttempts $using:ATTEMPTS -BaseDelayMs 250

  [pscustomobject]@{
    i      = [int]$i
    ok     = [bool]$r.ok
    status = $r.status
    cache  = $r.cache
    msg    = $r.msg
  }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..100) -Script $script -Throttle $THROTTLE

$okCount   = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count
$failCount = @($results | Where-Object { -not $_.ok }).Count

# status buckets
$bucket = @{}
foreach ($x in $results) {
  $k = if ($x.ok) { "200" } elseif ($null -eq $x.status) { "null" } else { [string]$x.status }
  if (-not $bucket.ContainsKey($k)) { $bucket[$k] = 0 }
  $bucket[$k]++
}
$bucketLine = ($bucket.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Value)" }) -join " "

$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE2_V41 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) throttle=$THROTTLE timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT attempts=$ATTEMPTS"
$lines = @()
$lines += $meta
$lines += "SUMMARY ok=$okCount fail=$failCount buckets: $bucketLine"
$lines += ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object {
  if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK 200 {1}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.cache) { $_.cache } else { "" }) }
  else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.status) { $_.status } else { "null" }), $_.msg }
})

Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines
Get-Content $logPath | Select-String "SUMMARY"
'@ -Encoding utf8

# RUN stage2
. .\stage2-injection.ps1

# If you want the full summary line again:

That means your current common.ps1 does not contain Invoke-WithRetry (Stage2_V41 calls it, but the background jobs only see what common.ps1 defines). Fix = add the helper into tools\aidut\common.ps1, then rerun Stage2. ✅

Run this from C:\phoenix\tools\aidut:

cd C:\phoenix\tools\aidut

# Append the missing V4.1 retry helper into common.ps1 (safe + minimal)
Add-Content .\common.ps1 -Encoding utf8 -Value @'

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# V4.1 retry helper (used by Stage2)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function Invoke-WithRetry {
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Fn,
    [int]$MaxAttempts = 4,
    [int]$BaseDelayMs = 250
  )

  for ($attempt = 1; $attempt -le $MaxAttempts; $attempt++) {
    $r = & $Fn
    if ($r -and $r.ok) { return $r }

    $status = $null
    try { $status = [int]$r.status } catch {}

    # Retry on: timeout/unknown (status null), 429, 503, 504, and transient web errors
    $retryable = $false
    if ($null -eq $status) { $retryable = $true }
    elseif ($status -in 429,503,504) { $retryable = $true }
    elseif ($r.msg -match "timed out|timeout|temporarily|connection|An error occurred") { $retryable = $true }

    if (-not $retryable -or $attempt -eq $MaxAttempts) { return $r }

    # exponential backoff + jitter
    $jitter = Get-Random -Minimum 0 -Maximum 200
    $delay = [Math]::Min(5000, ($BaseDelayMs * [Math]::Pow(2, ($attempt-1))) + $jitter)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $delay
  }
}
'@

# Sanity check: should print the function name
Get-Command Invoke-WithRetry

# Ensure workerurl exists (Stage2 needs it)
mkdir .\proof -Force | Out-Null
"https://phoenix-empire.mrmichaelhobbs123.workers.dev" | Set-Content .\proof\workerurl.txt -NoNewline

# Tunables (recommended for PS 5.1)
$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_THROTTLE   = "20"
$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC = "25"
$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_ATTEMPTS   = "4"

# Re-run Stage2
. .\stage2-injection.ps1

# Show the SUMMARY line
Get-Content .\proof\stage2-injection.txt | Select-String "SUMMARY"

Perfect. That’s the cleanest possible outcome for Stage2. 🎯

Your summary tells us everything:

SUMMARY ok=18 fail=82 buckets: 200=18 409=82

Interpretation (important):

This is NOT saturation, not timeouts, not 429/503/504.
All “fails” are 409 conflicts, which in your system are dedupe / replay protection (same nonce / same idempotency constraint) doing its job.

Stage2 as currently written is effectively exercising “write-collision under concurrency,” not “retry under transient failure.” Because 409 is deterministic, retries won’t help (and shouldn’t).

So V4.1 is “working,” but it’s measuring a different thing than your original intent (high-concurrency injection survival). With 18 writes accepted and 82 rejected as duplicates/conflicts, the ledger is behaving like a protected append-only system should.

Now the immediate fix is: decide what Stage2 is supposed to prove.

If Stage2 goal is “high-concurrency throughput without tripping dedupe,” then change the test to guarantee uniqueness in the dedupe keyspace.

Do this next (minimal surgical patch):

A) Make block_id unique per request run (not just INJ-$i), and make nonce unique per request + run.
B) If the worker’s conflict key includes command_id, also randomize it.

Set-Content .\stage2-injection.ps1 -Encoding utf8 -Value @'
. "$PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

$cfg = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg
$logPath = "proof/stage2-injection.txt"

$THROTTLE = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_THROTTLE) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_THROTTLE } else { 20 }
$TIMEOUT  = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_TIMEOUTSEC } else { 25 }
$ATTEMPTS = if ($env:AIDUT_STAGE2_ATTEMPTS) { [int]$env:AIDUT_STAGE2_ATTEMPTS } else { 4 }

# unique run id so block_id/command_id never collide across executions
$RUN = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ") + "-" + (Get-Random)

Write-Host "STAGE2_V41 start throttle=$THROTTLE timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT attempts=$ATTEMPTS run=$RUN url=$workerUrl path=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH)" -ForegroundColor Cyan

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot/common.ps1"

  $nonce = [int]$i + (Get-Random -Minimum 100000 -Maximum 999999)

  $body = @{
    ledger_id     = "audit-stage2"
    block_id      = "INJ-$using:RUN-$i"
    timestamp_utc = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
    author        = "AIDUT"
    mode          = "EXECUTION"
    block_type    = "TEST"
    payload       = @{ seq = [int]$i; run = "$using:RUN" }
    nonce         = $nonce
    protocol      = "PHOENIX/1.3"
    command_id    = "inj-$using:RUN-$i"
  }

  $fn = {
    Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec $using:TIMEOUT
  }

  $r = Invoke-WithRetry -Fn $fn -MaxAttempts $using:ATTEMPTS -BaseDelayMs 250

  [pscustomobject]@{
    i      = [int]$i
    ok     = [bool]$r.ok
    status = $r.status
    cache  = $r.cache
    msg    = $r.msg
  }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..100) -Script $script -Throttle $THROTTLE

$okCount   = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count
$failCount = @($results | Where-Object { -not $_.ok }).Count

$bucket = @{}
foreach ($x in $results) {
  $k = if ($x.ok) { "200" } elseif ($null -eq $x.status) { "null" } else { [string]$x.status }
  if (-not $bucket.ContainsKey($k)) { $bucket[$k] = 0 }
  $bucket[$k]++
}
$bucketLine = ($bucket.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Value)" }) -join " "

$meta = "AIDUT_STAGE2_V41 workerUrl=$workerUrl appendPath=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH) throttle=$THROTTLE timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT attempts=$ATTEMPTS run=$RUN"
$lines = @()
$lines += $meta
$lines += "SUMMARY ok=$okCount fail=$failCount buckets: $bucketLine"
$lines += ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object {
  if ($_.ok) { "{0} OK 200 {1}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.cache) { $_.cache } else { "" }) }
  else { "{0} FAIL {1} {2}" -f $_.i, $(if ($_.status) { $_.status } else { "null" }), $_.msg }
})

Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines
Get-Content $logPath | Select-String "SUMMARY"
'@

# run Stage2 again
. .\stage2-injection.ps1
What you should see after this: buckets dominated by 200, and if anything fails it’ll be the “real” failures (timeouts / 429 / 503 / null). That’s the signal you originally wanted.

Got it. This result is actually a very strong signal:

SUMMARY ok=5 fail=95 buckets: 200=5 409=95

If you’re still getting 95% 409 even after making block_id + command_id + payload.run + nonce unique, then 409 is not dedupe on (nonce/block_id/command_id) in this worker. It’s almost certainly being used as write-contention/backpressure (e.g., SQLite locked / DO busy / “conflict” return path under concurrent writes). ✅

So we do two things, in order:

Make the tooling print the response body for 409 so we know what 409 means (DUPLICATE vs SQLITE_BUSY vs LOCKED vs RATE).

Tune Stage2 for write contention (lower throttle + retry 409 with jitter), because if it’s SQLITE_BUSY, retrying is correct.

Step 1 — Patch common.ps1 to capture error response body (critical)
From C:\phoenix\tools\aidut:

# Replace Invoke-LedgerAppend in common.ps1 with a version that returns response body on failures
# (safe to paste: it only changes the catch block behavior)

(Get-Content .\common.ps1 -Raw) | Set-Content .\common.ps1 -Encoding utf8

# Manually edit: easiest is to re-Set-Content the whole function. Do this:
Set-Content .\_invoke_patch.ps1 -Encoding utf8 -Value @'
function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$AppendPath,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Headers,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Body,
    [int]$TimeoutSec = 5
  )
  Assert-NotUnknown -Value $AppendPath -Name "APPEND_PATH"
  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec -UseBasicParsing
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=$res.Headers["x-cache"]; msg="OK"; body=$null }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    $respBody = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    try {
      $stream = $_.Exception.Response.GetResponseStream()
      if ($stream) {
        $reader = New-Object System.IO.StreamReader($stream)
        $respBody = $reader.ReadToEnd()
      }
    } catch {}
    $m = $_.Exception.Message.Split(":")[0]
    if ($respBody) { $m = "$m | body=$respBody" }
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=$m; body=$respBody }
  }
}
'@
