Set-Content .\common.v41.ps1 -Encoding utf8 -Value @'
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

function Get-WorkerUrl {
  param([string]$Path = "$PSScriptRoot\proof\workerurl.txt")
  if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { throw "MISSING: $Path" }
  $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim()
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }
  return $u.TrimEnd("/")
}

function Get-AidutConfig {
  $cfg = [ordered]@{
    AUTH_SCHEME      = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME } else { "header" }
    AUTH_HEADER_NAME = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME } else { "x-sovereign-key" }
    AUTH_KEY         = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY } else { "MISSING_ENV_AIDUT_AUTH_KEY" }
    APPEND_PATH      = if ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH) { $env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH } else { "/ledger/append" }
  }
  if ($cfg.AUTH_KEY -match "^MISSING_ENV") { throw "Set `$env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY first." }
  return $cfg
}

function New-AidutHeaders {
  param([hashtable]$Cfg)
  $h = @{ "content-type" = "application/json" }
  if ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -eq "bearer") { $h["Authorization"] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)" }
  elseif ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -eq "header") { $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY }
  else { throw "AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be bearer|header (got $($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME))" }
  return $h
}

function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$WorkerUrl,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$AppendPath,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Headers,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][hashtable]$Body,
    [int]$TimeoutSec = 10
  )
  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec -UseBasicParsing
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=$res.Headers["x-cache"]; msg="OK"; body=$null }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    $respBody = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    try {
      $stream = $_.Exception.Response.GetResponseStream()
      if ($stream) {
        $reader = New-Object System.IO.StreamReader($stream)
        $respBody = $reader.ReadToEnd()
      }
    } catch {}
    $m = $_.Exception.Message.Split(":")[0]
    if ($respBody) { $m = "$m | body=$respBody" }
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=$m; body=$respBody }
  }
}

function Invoke-WithRetry {
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Fn,
    [int]$MaxAttempts = 8,
    [int]$BaseDelayMs = 250
  )
  for ($attempt=1; $attempt -le $MaxAttempts; $attempt++) {
    $r = & $Fn
    if ($r -and $r.ok) { return $r }

    $status = $null
    try { $status = [int]$r.status } catch {}

    $retryable = $false
    if ($null -eq $status) { $retryable = $true }
    elseif ($status -in 409,429,503,504) { $retryable = $true }
    elseif ($r.msg -match "timed out|timeout|temporarily|connection|An error occurred") { $retryable = $true }

    if (-not $retryable -or $attempt -eq $MaxAttempts) { return $r }

    $jitter = Get-Random -Minimum 0 -Maximum 200
    $delay = [Math]::Min(5000, ($BaseDelayMs * [Math]::Pow(2, ($attempt-1))) + $jitter)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $delay
  }
}

function Write-DeterministicLog {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path, [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][object[]]$Rows)
  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent
  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
  ($Rows | ForEach-Object { [string]$_ }) | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8
}

function Invoke-Concurrent {
  param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int[]]$Inputs,
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Script,
    [int]$Throttle = 5
  )
  $jobs = @()
  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {
    $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i
    if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) {
      $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output
      $jobs = @()
    }
  }
  if ($jobs.Count -gt 0) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output }
}
'@
Create stage2.v41.ps1

Set-Content .\stage2.v41.ps1 -Encoding utf8 -Value @'
. "$PSScriptRoot\common.v41.ps1"

# REQUIRED envs (do not paste secrets into chat)
# $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY = "<your key>"
$env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME = "header"
$env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME = "x-sovereign-key"
$env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH = "/ledger/append"

$cfg = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg

$THROTTLE = 5
$TIMEOUT  = 35
$ATTEMPTS = 8

$RUN = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ") + "-" + (Get-Random)
$logPath = "$PSScriptRoot\proof\stage2-v41.txt"

Write-Host "STAGE2_V41 throttle=$THROTTLE timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT attempts=$ATTEMPTS run=$RUN" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "url=$workerUrl path=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH)" -ForegroundColor Cyan

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot\common.v41.ps1"

  $nonce = [int]$i + (Get-Random -Minimum 100000 -Maximum 999999)

  $body = @{
    ledger_id     = "audit-stage2"
    block_id      = "INJ-$using:RUN-$i"
    timestamp_utc = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
    author        = "AIDUT"
    mode          = "EXECUTION"
    block_type    = "TEST"
    payload       = @{ seq = [int]$i; run = "$using:RUN" }
    nonce         = $nonce
    protocol      = "PHOENIX/1.3"
    command_id    = "inj-$using:RUN-$i"
  }

  $fn = { Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec $using:TIMEOUT }
  $r = Invoke-WithRetry -Fn $fn -MaxAttempts $using:ATTEMPTS -BaseDelayMs 250

  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=[bool]$r.ok; status=$r.status; msg=$r.msg }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..100) -Script $script -Throttle $THROTTLE
$ok  = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count
$bad = @($results | Where-Object { -not $_.ok }).Count

$bucket = @{}
foreach ($x in $results) {
  $k = if ($x.ok) { "200" } elseif ($null -eq $x.status) { "null" } else { [string]$x.status }
  if (-not $bucket.ContainsKey($k)) { $bucket[$k] = 0 }
  $bucket[$k]++
}
$bucketLine = ($bucket.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Value)" }) -join " "

$lines = @()
$lines += "SUMMARY ok=$ok fail=$bad buckets: $bucketLine"
$lines += ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { "$($_.i) OK 200" } else { "$($_.i) FAIL $($_.status) $($_.msg)" } })

Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines
Get-Content $logPath | Select-String "SUMMARY"
'@

BLOCK 1: CREATE tools/aidut/common.v41.ps1
(The Core Engine with Retries & Body Capture)

PowerShell
# Create directory if missing
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path "tools/aidut" -Force | Out-Null

Set-Content "tools/aidut/common.v41.ps1" -Encoding utf8 -Value @'
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

function Get-WorkerUrl {
  param([string]$Path = "$PSScriptRoot\proof\workerurl.txt")
  if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { 
      # Fallback check relative to current dir
      if (Test-Path "proof\workerurl.txt") { $Path = "proof\workerurl.txt" }
      else { throw "MISSING: $Path" }
  }
  $u = (Get-Content $Path -Raw).Trim()
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($u)) { throw "EMPTY worker url in $Path" }
  return $u.TrimEnd("/")
}

function Get-AidutConfig {
  $cfg = [ordered]@{
    AUTH_SCHEME      = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME } else { "header" }
    AUTH_HEADER_NAME = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME } else { "x-sovereign-key" }
    AUTH_KEY         = if ($env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY) { $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY } else { "MISSING_ENV_AIDUT_AUTH_KEY" }
    APPEND_PATH      = if ($env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH) { $env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH } else { "/ledger/append" }
  }
  if ($cfg.AUTH_KEY -match "^MISSING_ENV") { throw "Set $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY first." }
  return $cfg
}

function New-AidutHeaders {
  param([hashtable]$Cfg)
  $h = @{ "content-type" = "application/json" }
  if ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -eq "bearer") { $h["Authorization"] = "Bearer $($Cfg.AUTH_KEY)" }
  elseif ($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME -eq "header") { $h[$Cfg.AUTH_HEADER_NAME] = $Cfg.AUTH_KEY }
  else { throw "AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME must be bearer|header (got $($Cfg.AUTH_SCHEME))" }
  return $h
}

function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  param(
    [string]$WorkerUrl,
    [string]$AppendPath,
    [hashtable]$Headers,
    [hashtable]$Body,
    [int]$TimeoutSec = 10
  )
  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$WorkerUrl$AppendPath" -Method POST -Headers $Headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec -UseBasicParsing
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; cache=$res.Headers["x-cache"]; msg="OK"; body=$null }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    $respBody = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    try {
      $stream = $_.Exception.Response.GetResponseStream()
      if ($stream) {
        $reader = New-Object System.IO.StreamReader($stream)
        $respBody = $reader.ReadToEnd()
      }
    } catch {}
    $m = $_.Exception.Message.Split(":")[0]
    if ($respBody) { $m = "$m | body=$respBody" }
    return [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; cache=$null; msg=$m; body=$respBody }
  }
}

function Invoke-WithRetry {
  param(
    [scriptblock]$Fn,
    [int]$MaxAttempts = 8,
    [int]$BaseDelayMs = 250
  )
  for ($attempt=1; $attempt -le $MaxAttempts; $attempt++) {
    $r = & $Fn
    if ($r -and $r.ok) { return $r }

    $status = $null
    try { $status = [int]$r.status } catch {}

    $retryable = $false
    if ($null -eq $status) { $retryable = $true }
    elseif ($status -in 409,429,503,504) { $retryable = $true }
    elseif ($r.msg -match "timed out|timeout|temporarily|connection|An error occurred") { $retryable = $true }

    if (-not $retryable -or $attempt -eq $MaxAttempts) { return $r }

    $jitter = Get-Random -Minimum 0 -Maximum 200
    $delay = [Math]::Min(5000, ($BaseDelayMs * [Math]::Pow(2, ($attempt-1))) + $jitter)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $delay
  }
  return $r
}

function Write-DeterministicLog {
  param([string]$Path, [object[]]$Rows)
  $dir = Split-Path $Path -Parent
  if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
  ($Rows | ForEach-Object { [string]$_ }) | Set-Content -Path $Path -Encoding utf8
}

function Invoke-Concurrent {
  param(
    [int[]]$Inputs,
    [scriptblock]$Script,
    [int]$Throttle = 5
  )
  $jobs = @()
  foreach ($i in $Inputs) {
    $jobs += Start-Job -ScriptBlock $Script -ArgumentList $i
    if ($jobs.Count -ge $Throttle) {
      $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output
      $jobs = @()
    }
  }
  if ($jobs.Count -gt 0) { $jobs | Receive-Job -Wait -AutoRemoveJob | Write-Output }
}
'@
BLOCK 2: CREATE tools/aidut/stage2.v41.ps1
(The Tuned Runner: Low Throttle, Unique Run ID)

PowerShell
Set-Content "tools/aidut/stage2.v41.ps1" -Encoding utf8 -Value @'
. "$PSScriptRoot\common.v41.ps1"

# CONFIG
$env:AIDUT_AUTH_SCHEME = "header"
$env:AIDUT_AUTH_HEADER_NAME = "x-sovereign-key"
$env:AIDUT_APPEND_PATH = "/ledger/append"

$cfg = Get-AidutConfig
$workerUrl = Get-WorkerUrl
$headers = New-AidutHeaders -Cfg $cfg

$THROTTLE = 5
$TIMEOUT  = 35
$ATTEMPTS = 8

$RUN = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ") + "-" + (Get-Random)
$logPath = "$PSScriptRoot\..\..\proof\stage2-v41.txt"

Write-Host "STAGE2_V41 throttle=$THROTTLE timeoutSec=$TIMEOUT attempts=$ATTEMPTS run=$RUN" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "url=$workerUrl path=$($cfg.APPEND_PATH)" -ForegroundColor Cyan

$script = {
  param($i)
  . "$using:PSScriptRoot\common.v41.ps1"

  $nonce = [int]$i + (Get-Random -Minimum 100000 -Maximum 999999)

  $body = @{
    ledger_id     = "audit-stage2"
    block_id      = "INJ-$using:RUN-$i"
    timestamp_utc = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
    author        = "AIDUT"
    mode          = "EXECUTION"
    block_type    = "TEST"
    payload       = @{ seq = [int]$i; run = "$using:RUN" }
    nonce         = $nonce
    protocol      = "PHOENIX/1.3"
    command_id    = "inj-$using:RUN-$i"
  }

  $fn = { Invoke-LedgerAppend -WorkerUrl $using:workerUrl -AppendPath $using:cfg.APPEND_PATH -Headers $using:headers -Body $body -TimeoutSec $using:TIMEOUT }
  $r = Invoke-WithRetry -Fn $fn -MaxAttempts $using:ATTEMPTS -BaseDelayMs 250

  [pscustomobject]@{ i=[int]$i; ok=[bool]$r.ok; status=$r.status; msg=$r.msg }
}

$results = Invoke-Concurrent -Inputs (1..100) -Script $script -Throttle $THROTTLE
$ok  = @($results | Where-Object ok).Count
$bad = @($results | Where-Object { -not $_.ok }).Count

$bucket = @{}
foreach ($x in $results) {
  $k = if ($x.ok) { "200" } elseif ($null -eq $x.status) { "null" } else { [string]$x.status }
  if (-not $bucket.ContainsKey($k)) { $bucket[$k] = 0 }
  $bucket[$k]++
}
$bucketLine = ($bucket.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Value)" }) -join " "

$lines = @()
$lines += "SUMMARY ok=$ok fail=$bad buckets: $bucketLine"
$lines += ($results | Sort-Object i | ForEach-Object { if ($_.ok) { "$($_.i) OK 200" } else { "$($_.i) FAIL $($_.status) $($_.msg)" } })

Write-DeterministicLog -Path $logPath -Rows $lines
Get-Content $logPath | Select-String "SUMMARY"
'@

Step 1 — SIMPLE core helper (single file)
Save as: C:\phoenix\tools\aidut\aidut.simple.ps1

Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

function Get-WorkerUrl {
  $p1 = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "proof\workerurl.txt"
  $p2 = "C:\phoenix\proof\workerurl.txt"
  if (Test-Path $p1) { return (Get-Content $p1 -Raw).Trim().TrimEnd("/") }
  if (Test-Path $p2) { return (Get-Content $p2 -Raw).Trim().TrimEnd("/") }
  throw "MISSING workerurl.txt (expected $p1 or $p2)"
}

function Invoke-LedgerAppend {
  param(
    [string]$Url,
    [string]$Path = "/ledger/append",
    [string]$HeaderName = "x-sovereign-key",
    [string]$Key,
    [hashtable]$Body,
    [int]$TimeoutSec = 20
  )

  $json = $Body | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
  $headers = @{ "content-type" = "application/json"; $HeaderName = $Key }

  try {
    $res = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri ($Url + $Path) -Method POST -Headers $headers -Body $json -TimeoutSec $TimeoutSec -UseBasicParsing
    [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$true; status=[int]$res.StatusCode; msg="OK"; body=$null }
  } catch {
    $status = $null
    $respBody = $null
    try { $status = $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__ } catch {}
    try {
      $s = $_.Exception.Response.GetResponseStream()
      if ($s) { $respBody = (New-Object IO.StreamReader($s)).ReadToEnd() }
    } catch {}
    $m = $_.Exception.Message.Split(":")[0]
    if ($respBody) { $m = "$m | body=$respBody" }
    [pscustomobject]@{ ok=$false; status=$status; msg=$m; body=$respBody }
  }
}

if (-not $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY) { throw "Set `$env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY in your terminal first." }

$u = Get-WorkerUrl
$run = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ") + "-" + (Get-Random)

$results = @()
foreach ($i in 1..25) {
  $body = @{
    ledger_id     = "audit-stage2"
    block_id      = "INJ-$run-$i"
    timestamp_utc = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
    author        = "AIDUT"
    mode          = "EXECUTION"
    block_type    = "TEST"
    payload       = @{ seq = $i; run = $run }
    nonce         = (Get-Random -Minimum 100000000 -Maximum 999999999)
    protocol      = "PHOENIX/1.3"
    command_id    = "inj-$run-$i"
  }

  $r = Invoke-LedgerAppend -Url $u -Key $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY -Body $body -TimeoutSec 25
  $results += [pscustomobject]@{ i=$i; ok=$r.ok; status=$r.status; msg=$r.msg }
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200
}

$ok = @($results | ? ok).Count
$bucket = $results | Group-Object status | Sort-Object Name | % { "$($_.Name)=$($_.Count)" } -join " "
"SUMMARY ok=$ok total=$($results.Count) buckets: $bucket"

# show the first failure line (includes body if 409)
$firstFail = $results | ? { -not $_.ok } | Select-Object -First 1
if ($firstFail) {
  "FIRST_FAIL: i=$($firstFail.i) status=$($firstFail.status)"
  # re-send that same request index once and print full body capture
  $i = [int]$firstFail.i
  $body = @{
    ledger_id     = "audit-stage2"
    block_id      = "INJ-$run-REPRO-$i"
    timestamp_utc = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o")
    author        = "AIDUT"
    mode          = "EXECUTION"
    block_type    = "TEST"
    payload       = @{ seq = $i; run = $run; repro = $true }
    nonce         = (Get-Random -Minimum 100000000 -Maximum 999999999)
    protocol      = "PHOENIX/1.3"
    command_id    = "inj-$run-REPRO-$i"
  }
  $full = Invoke-LedgerAppend -Url $u -Key $env:AIDUT_AUTH_KEY -Body $body -TimeoutSec 25
  $full | Format-List ok,status,msg,body
}

It assumes Stage 0 (installing and authenticating Codex CLI) was completed, and leverages your newly enabled Google Drive and GitHub connectors only if needed for internal documentation.

✔️ Stage 1 Overview: Repository Integration
This stage prepares your project repository so Codex can work predictably and safely. It introduces a configuration file for Codex, defines commands Codex can run, and ensures no sensitive data or unwanted operations occur. All steps are executed locally in PowerShell.

🔧 1. Verify Environment Setup (Quick Check)
Ensure you are in the correct project directory and have the necessary tools installed:

node --version     # confirm Node.js installed

If any of these commands fail, install the missing tool before proceeding.

📂 2. Create .codex Configuration Directory
Codex reads its configuration from a .codex folder at the root of your repository:

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Name ".codex" -Force | Out-Null
🛠️ 3. Create or Update .codex/config.toml
This file controls how Codex runs in your project. You can start with a minimal configuration and expand it as you develop. Open your favourite editor to add the following:

# .codex/config.toml
sandbox_mode = "workspace-write"     # Codex can write files, but only within your repo
approvals = "on-request"             # Codex asks for approval before running commands
max_iterations = 3                   # Limit how many auto-refinements Codex can attempt
Add other settings (such as project_name or ignore_files) later if needed.

🧠 4. Define AGENTS.md (Allowed Commands)
Codex uses this file to understand what commands it is allowed to run in your project. It is stored at the repository root and should list common workflows, such as installing dependencies or running tests. Create it with:

# AGENTS.md

## Approved Commands

- Install dependencies: `npm ci`
- Build project: `npm run build`
- Lint code: `npm run lint`
- Run unit tests: `npm test`

## Notes

- Use `npm` for all package management (avoid `yarn` or `pnpm` here).
- Do not modify `env` files or production secrets.
- Ask before committing any changes that affect CI/CD workflows.
Feel free to expand this list with additional project‑specific commands (e.g., npm run start-dev or dotnet run), but keep the guidelines explicit.

🔐 5. Protect Sensitive Data
Before letting Codex operate on your repository, ensure that secrets are not in plain text. Check for .env or config files that should be ignored by Codex:

New-Item -ItemType File -Name .gitignore -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Out-Null
Add-Content .gitignore "`n.env`"
Add-Content .gitignore ".codex/tmp/"
You can also list additional files or directories that Codex should never touch in .codex/config.toml under ignore_files.

📑 6. (Optional) Fetch Internal Guidelines via Connectors

Stage 2: Continuous Integration & Automation
Create or update a GitHub workflow

In the root of your deathstar-phase1 repository, create .github/workflows/codex.yml.

This workflow triggers on pull requests and runs Codex in a “review” mode so it can analyze the PR and make suggestions before merging.

A basic template:

name: Codex PR Check
on:
  pull_request:
    types: [opened, synchronize]
jobs:
  codex-review:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      - run: npm ci
      - run: npm run build    # or your build command
      - run: codex review --approve --config .codex/config.toml
Adjust the commands (npm ci, npm run build, etc.) to match those you listed in your AGENTS.md.

Add Codex commands to CI

Besides PR checks, you can add tasks such as linting and testing as separate jobs in the same workflow.

Use your AGENTS.md definitions (e.g., npm run lint or npm test) so Codex has explicit, allowed commands.

Expand .codex/config.toml for CI

Include these keys in your config to fine‑tune CI behavior:

[ci]
run_on_pull_request = true          # run Codex on each PR
run_on_push = false                 # optional: disable on push
allow_experimentation = false       # no auto‑refactoring on CI runs
You can also set skip_if_no_diff = true so CI skips Codex if nothing changed in relevant files.
